# Item physics family audit

Generated by `tools/item_physics_audit.py` (`/cegen run item_physics_audit`). Do not edit by hand.
Design and the family table: [item_physics.md](item_physics.md) section 9. Block material and fire
resistance come from `/dumpphysics`: **not dumped yet**, so block items are classified by tag and name only and the lava list is partial.

Rungs, first match wins: `tag` (a vanilla/forge/pack tag) > `fire_resistant` (the item's own flag) >
`material` (block Material, refined by SoundType) > `gear` (tool tier / armor material) > `edible` > `name`
(a token in the id) > `mod_default` > `none` (unclassified). "Manual" = already in one of the four physics
tags. "Differs" = the family's behaviour is not what the item does now.
